# Shared holder-to-domaine links

Holder research is done once per legal holder, not once per cru. Each link connects a recorded legal holder (SIREN or provisional DGFiP identifier) to a domaine or producer through a stated company relation and its sources. It is shown as **Research link · farming unverified**: a relation between companies never establishes who farms a parcel. Legal holder, applicant, operator and bottler stay distinct.

Generated by `python scripts/build_grand_cru_holder_links.py`; use `--check` to verify. Edit [the table](holder-links.json), not this report. Method: [Grand Cru parcel rollout, shared holder research](../../grand-cru-parcel-rollout.md#shared-holder-research).

## Coverage

377 recorded holders across 33 crus; 168 hold rights in more than one cru. 1,579 distinct parcels have recorded rights; the 50 holders with the most parcels hold rights on 748 of them (47%).

The table has 264 holders: 134 reviewed, 27 provisional and 0 retired links; 107 holders were searched without finding a link. Holders with an active link hold rights on 802 distinct parcels (51%), before any cru scope is applied.

A cru uses the table once its curation sets `"holderLinks": "shared"`, normally in its Tier 2 PR. "Reached" counts parcels where at least one recorded holder has a link applying to that cru, whether or not the cru uses the table yet; a link alone is not a lead to a farmer.

## Link evidence

Each link with its basis and sources. Retired links stay listed with their reason and are never shown in the app.

- **200047827/domaine-des-hospices-de-beaune** — Domaine des Hospices de Beaune; owner-company (the holder is the domaine's own company); reviewed 2026-10-07. The recorded holder is the Hospices Civils de Beaune itself, whose estate sells its cuvées as the Domaine des Hospices de Beaune. Ownership is not farming of any parcel. Sources: rne-200047827.
- **778269407/domaine-de-la-romanee-conti** — Domaine de la Romanée-Conti; owner-company (the holder is the domaine's own company); reviewed 2026-10-07. The recorded holder is the société civile Domaine de la Romanée-Conti (vine growing, Vosne-Romanée). Sources: register-drc.
- **775567928/domaine-faiveley** — Domaine Faiveley; brand-identity (the company behind the domaine's brand); reviewed 2026-10-07. Faiveley's own legal notice gives SAS CVVB, RCS Dijon 775567928, as the company behind the brand. Sources: faiveley-identity, rne-775567928.
- **515620466/domaine-capitain-gagnerot** — Domaine Capitain-Gagnerot; owner-company (the holder is the domaine's own company); reviewed 2026-10-07. The company’s own legal notice gives SIREN 515620466 for Maison Capitain Gagnerot, a vine-growing SAS in Ladoix-Serrigny; the estate trades as Domaine Capitain-Gagnerot. Sources: rne-515620466, capitain-legal.
- **429825250** — searched 2026-10-09 in https://recherche-entreprises.api.gouv.fr/search?q=429825250, https://entreprises.lefigaro.fr/domaine-georges-lignier-pere-et-fils-21/entreprise-429825250; no link found. This legal holder is a GFA. Its 1976 schedule matches AB68/130, with historical fractional interests retained. Later future-lease authority does not prove the named operating company leases these parcels.
- **515420255/bouchard-pere-fils** — Bouchard Père & Fils; parent-group (a company that controls the domaine); reviewed 2026-10-07. Recorded as Maison Bouchard Père et Fils; in 2026 it contributed a business branch to NOMINOE (981 574 650) for 3,142,126 of its 3,142,858 shares and was renamed Vignoble des Cabottes. 981 574 650 is now Bouchard Père et Fils, the company in Bouchard’s legal notice; both are presided by Artémis Domaines. Sources: rne-515420255, bodacc-bouchard-2026, rne-981574650, bouchard-legal.
- **U21852238 → SIREN 427468970** (identity crosswalk). The statutes’ 1972 schedule names 16 of this identifier’s 19 Corton parcels with matching cadastral areas, and the register name and office (Château de Corton-Grancey, Aloxe-Corton) agree. The rights file keeps the provisional identifier. Sources: rne-427468970, corton-grancey-statutes.
- **U21852238/domaine-louis-latour** — Domaine Louis Latour; lessor-per-filing (a filing names the domaine side as tenant or intended tenant); reviewed 2026-10-07. Limited to charlemagne, corton, corton-charlemagne, romanee-saint-vivant. Lease: recited. The GFA’s statutes recite that all contributed land is let to the Société Civile Domaine Louis Latour (Château Corton-Grancey) under a 1966 lease renewed in 1998 and on 3 June 2013 to 31 October 2031. The renewal deeds themselves are not filed. Sources: corton-grancey-statutes, rne-778159715.
- **778269498/domaine-mongeard-mugneret** — Domaine Mongeard-Mugneret; family-holding (a family land company of the domaine's owners); reviewed 2026-10-07. The GFA Mongeard-Mugneret et Fils (rue de la Fontaine, Vosne-Romanée) was founded in 1964 by contribution of family vineyards; the operating SA is at 14 rue de la Fontaine. Its statutes require leasing but name no tenant. Sources: register-mongeard-gfa, mongeard-gfa-1964.
- **528291362/maison-louis-latour** — Maison Louis Latour; subsidiary (a company the domaine controls); reviewed 2026-10-07. Maison Louis Latour holds 54,999 of the company’s 55,000 shares after contributing one-third shares of its Corton vineyards on 28 September 2011, and is a managing partner with the Corton-Grancey GFA. Sources: vignoble-latour-2011, rne-528291362, rne-515720076.
- **036380046/domaine-du-clos-frantin-albert-bichot** — Domaine du Clos Frantin / Albert Bichot; owner-company (the holder is the domaine's own company); reviewed 2026-10-07. Limited to echezeaux, grands-echezeaux. Bichot SA is the Albert Bichot company. Domaine du Clos Frantin is the label Bichot publishes for its Vosne-Romanée and Flagey holdings, so this link is limited to these two crus. Sources: register-bichot.
- **036380046/domaine-du-clos-frantin-albert-bichot-clos-de-vougeot** — Domaine du Clos Frantin / Albert Bichot; owner-company (the holder is the domaine's own company); reviewed 2026-10-07. Limited to clos-de-vougeot. Bichot SA is the Albert Bichot company; merchants sell its Clos de Vougeot as Domaine du Clos Frantin, the label it also uses in Vosne-Romanée and Flagey. Sources: register-bichot, caviste-clos-frantin-vougeot.
- **036380046/richebourg** — Domaine du Clos Frantin / Albert Bichot; owner-company (the holder is the domaine's own company); reviewed 2026-10-08. Limited to richebourg. Sources: rne-036380046, ric-bichot-richebourg-sheet.

## Sources

- **arnoux-legal** — [Domaine Arnoux Père et Fils: legal notice](https://www.domaine-arnoux.com/mentions-legales/). estate; document date not established; reviewed 2026-10-07. - **beaumonts-gfa-2008** — [GFA des Beaumonts: 21 November 2008 founding deed](https://actes.ccm2.net/acte/21ccf271-a107-4a80-826a-a78e3941087f#page=14). company-filing; document date 2008-11-21; reviewed 2026-10-07. Contributions include Ladoix-Serrigny AK 47 (Corton grand cru, 18 a 90 ca) and AK 49 (39 a 82 ca). Both are recited as let to S.A.R.L. Domaine Maillard Père et Fils: AK 47 under a 1982 lease tacitly prolonged to 31 December 2008, AK 49 under a lease of 12 December 1996 for 11 November 1996 to 10 November 2014.
- **belgrand-latour-statutes** — [GFA Domaine Belgrand-Latour: statutes updated 17 May 2025](https://actes.ccm2.net/acte/4a921f53-c95f-439c-bc02-43f219827e10#page=5). company-filing; document date 1974-11; reviewed 2026-10-07. Founding contribution of Aloxe-Corton A 42 (Le Charlemagne), A 55 (Les Pougets), N 9 and N 10 (Les Chaumes) with areas. All contributed land is recited as let to the Société Civile Domaine Louis Latour: the 1966 lease, then a new 25-year lease signed 6 December 2001 for 11 November 2001 to 10 November 2026.
- **berthaut** — [Berthaut-Gerbet: estate history](https://www.berthaut-gerbet.com/). estate; document date not established; reviewed 2026-09-28. States the domaine combined all Berthaut vines with PART of Gerbet's vines in 2013. Cannot assign every Gerbet parcel from this statement.

- **modot-stat-2002** — [SCI Domaine Veuve Paul Modot: 2001 capital conversion and statutes](https://actes.ccm2.net/acte/6949588#page=1). company-filing; document date 2001-06-26; reviewed 2026-10-01. Same company abbreviation and office confirm the identity crosswalk. The founding 8 February 1964 contribution is cash, not a Grands-Échezeaux property schedule. No parcel-specific lease.
- **modot-stat-ccm** — [SCI Domaine Veuve Paul Modot: Schenk share changes and statutes](https://actes.ccm2.net/acte/3182750#page=1). company-filing; document date 2023-02-22; reviewed 2026-10-01. SIREN 778173500; Article 3 abbreviates the company as SCI Domaine Vve Paul MODOT, at 2 rue du Docteur Guyot, Savigny. The shares are held by Schenk Holding and Schenk Suisse after the Obrist merger. No Grands-Échezeaux cadastral schedule or lease.
- **mongeard-gfa-1964** — [Mongeard-Mugneret: founding contribution and 1997 GFA statutes](https://actes.ccm2.net/acte/5cd69d1a-b284-4aa8-85fc-5ea04e0d26e5#page=1). company-filing; document date 1964-02-12; reviewed 2026-10-08. The founding contribution schedule and its reproduction in the 1997 statutes print D535 (5,575 m²), D105 (290 m²), D104 (750 m²), total 6,615 m². Article 2 requires long-term leasing and forbids direct exploitation by the GFA; it does not identify an executed lease or tenant. Richebourg review: The original contribution and 1997 statutes name other vineyards, not current AN248. Mandatory long-term letting does not name a tenant; the estate’s 0.3112 ha publication remains census evidence only.
- **nicole** — [Official company search: Nicole Lamarche](https://recherche-entreprises.api.gouv.fr/search?q=397738634). registry; document date not established; reviewed 2026-09-28. Confirms the legal identity and active status, not farming.
- **orveaux-identity** — [ORVEAUX company record](https://www.pappers.fr/entreprise/orveaux-984543470). registry-aggregator; document date not established; reviewed 2026-09-28. Registry page describes ORVEAUX (984543470). The separately reviewed deed gives its full GFV name and four parcels, matching DGFiP holder U32852627.
- **orveaux-statutes-2024** — [ORVEAUX: founding deed and lease recitals](https://www.pappers.fr/entreprise/orveaux-984543470/documents/ORVEAUX%20-%20Copie%20des%20statuts%2012-02-2024.pdf). company-filing; document date 2024-02-09; reviewed 2026-09-29. The exact four-parcel contribution totals 5263 m²; the deed separately states 4147 m² plantable and planted, consistent with the rounded 0.41 ha importer figure. Laurent Jousset-Drouhin is the individual tenant, not a named operating company. The 2003 lease recital totals 4550 m² across three partial parcels and D0636 in full. The annex records tacit renewal but broadly calls the lease total; preserve the detailed scope.
- **pousse-echezeaux** — [North Berkeley Imports: Pousse d'Or Échezeaux sheet](https://www.northberkeleyimports.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/POUSSE_echezeaux.pdf). importer; document date not established; reviewed 2026-09-29. "Estate vines total a quarter-acre from lieu-dit 'Poullailères'"; the parcel "is not directly estate-owned, but managed by a holding company owned by" the estate's owner-winemaker. D0313 (0.11 ha, LES POULA) is ORIGINE's only parcel.

- **roumier-cc** — [Domaine Georges Roumier: Corton-Charlemagne](https://www.roumier.com/fr_FR/vin/corton-charlemagne). estate; document date not established; reviewed 2026-10-07. - **rue-des-chaumes-2025** — [Société Civile Rue des Chaumes: 9 September 2025 contribution deed](https://actes.ccm2.net/acte/1a49750c-7adb-4a5e-98d2-0ddda22d386e#page=1). company-filing; document date 2025-09-09; reviewed 2026-10-07. A contribution of Aloxe-Corton buildings to the SC, with SAS Philippe Senard (380 720 227) among the parties. It recites that the SC was formed in 1978 with Senard family contributions and that building parcel I 266 is under a nine-year commercial lease to SAS Philippe Senard from 1 April 2023. It names neither of the SC’s recorded Corton parcels (I 9, I 252).
- **saint-martin-statutes-2020** — [GFA Saint Martin: 2020 founding statutes (copy filed 2025)](https://actes.ccm2.net/acte/74f95cf0-87fd-4a55-9b1b-8f88940650b7#page=4). company-filing; document date 2020-07-30; reviewed 2026-10-07. The partners contribute undivided shares of Vougeot A54 (60 a 40 ca), received the same day from the capital reduction of Domaine Misset Chéron, which had held it since 1977. Page 29 mandates a 24-year rural lease to Domaine Misset Chéron (443879218).
- **sci-domaine-leroy-2001** — [SCI Domaine Leroy: 10 December 2001 minutes and original statutes](https://actes.ccm2.net/acte/1f10cb0c-c858-465c-b3ac-1d1c0b8cc1f8#page=1). company-filing; document date 2001-12-10; reviewed 2026-10-08. The SCI, formed in 1947 and registered in 2001, has Leroy SA and an individual as partners. Its original statutes value a Clos Vougeot vine under pre-renovation references; no current cadastral reference. Richebourg review: All pages screened for Richebourg: no exact AN57, AN61 or AN168 schedule. The match of SCI Domaine Leroy to U14149307 remains provisional on abbreviated name and seat. Romanée-Saint-Vivant review: every page screened again; the 1947 founding schedule lists Noëllat-family vines under pre-renovation section A references and names no Romanée-Saint-Vivant parcel; no AC 299 or AC 301 schedule.

- **sepv-stat-ccm** — [SEPV: 2022 board minutes and updated statutes](https://actes.ccm2.net/acte/81874e83-6d0e-46db-a084-b0fa72189ebb#page=3). company-filing; document date 2022-07-07; reviewed 2026-10-01. Article 2 names a majority participation in Maison Joseph Drouhin SA, SIREN 515620417. - **sordoillet-gfv-2009** — [GFV Marie Sordoillet: 2009 founding deed](https://actes.ccm2.net/acte/33491379#page=8). company-filing; document date 2009; reviewed 2026-10-07. They recite three long leases granted by the company to one of its partners: a bail à ferme and a métayage, both from 3 January 2000 to 3 January 2018, and a métayage from 1 November 2001 to 1 November 2019, so that the company does not farm directly. No parcel list is given for the leases; article six summarises the 1967 contributions by value only.

The 8 November 2001 métayage names AN 74, La Romanée, 84 a 52 ca (page image checked), for 18 years from 1 November 2001, tacitly renewable by nine-year periods.
